# Pitch shifting — polyphony, presets and DSP resources

[Repository](README.md) · [Guitar](all-guitar.md) · [Bass](all-bass.md) · [Vocal](all-vocal.md) · [Firmware](FIRMWARE-UPDATES.md) · [Pitch shifting](PITCH-SHIFTING.md)

**Checked through 2026-11-22. 330/402 active models have firmware audits; 330/402 have pitch audits; 0 supplemental/archival records are separate from active counts.** Each model is updated individually. Audited records can retain unresolved fields; this is not a claim that every specification is verified.

Polyphonic here concerns simultaneous input notes in the identified pitch algorithm. Stereo, two harmony voices, octave effects, synth polyphony and vocal pitch correction do not by themselves prove whole-chord transposition. Preset recall and the exact firmware/algorithm remain separate checks.

DSP percentages require the measurement basis, processor/path denominator, firmware and effect-chain configuration. Published specifications and owner measurements remain labelled separately. A fixed block count is not a CPU percentage, and unpublished resource costs are not zero. Values across unlike processors or denominators are not directly comparable.

Other named pitch choices include Pitch Wham, Twin Harmony, Simple Pitch, Dual Pitch, Boctaver and Legacy Smart Harmony/Bass Octaver; do not extend the Poly guarantee to those or to synth note tracking. Primary manufacturer online manuals and model list read directly. No firmware installation, preset mutation or audio tracking test. Existing exact-model inventory, older source qualifications and documented 46-processing-position template versus 48 block-location distinction retained. | 512 user preset locations, with separate read-only factory content. Built-in pedal defaults for Pitch Wham are distinct from Poly algorithm tracking. | Manufacturer hardware limit: two Poly/high-DSP effects per preset, one per primary path, sharing the class with Feedbacker, Poly Sustain and Poly Detune. Path 1 and Path 2 have independent DSP; 1A/1B share Path 1. No isolated pitch percentage published. Two effects maximum does not imply 50% each, and 48 block locations do not guarantee 48 heavy algorithms. Model choices are disabled when remaining DSP is insufficient; no device benchmark or transferred original Helix percentage. | [source 1](https://line6.com/helix-stadium-models/) [source 2](https://manuals.line6.com/en/helix-stadium/live/dynamic-dsp) [source 3](https://manuals.line6.com/en/helix-stadium/live/presets) [source 4](https://manuals.line6.com/en/helix-stadium/live/snapshots); checked 2026-11-22 |
